=== FILE: ./src/utils/sound.js ===
// Pure Web Audio API Sound Synthesizer (No external sound files required)
class SoundManager {
  constructor() {
    this.ctx = null;
    this.enabled = true;

    // Read preference from localStorage
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem('vq_sound_enabled');
      if (saved !== null) {
        this.enabled = saved === 'true';
      }
    }
  }

  initCtx() {
    if (!this.ctx && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
      if (AudioCtx) {
        this.ctx = new AudioCtx();
      }
    }
    if (this.ctx && this.ctx.state === 'suspended') {
      this.ctx.resume();
    }
  }

  isEnabled() {
    return this.enabled;
  }

  toggle() {
    this.enabled = !this.enabled;
    localStorage.setItem('vq_sound_enabled', String(this.enabled));
    if (this.enabled) {
      this.playClick();
    }
    return this.enabled;
  }

  setEnabled(val) {
    this.enabled = val;
    localStorage.setItem('vq_sound_enabled', String(val));
  }

  playClick() {
    if (!this.enabled) return;
    this.initCtx();
    if (!this.ctx) return;

    try {
      const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
      const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
      osc.type = 'triangle';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(1200, this.ctx.currentTime);
      osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(400, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.04);

      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.08, this.ctx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.04);

      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(this.ctx.destination);

      osc.start();
      osc.stop(this.ctx.currentTime + 0.04);
    } catch {
      // AudioContext policy fallback
    }
  }

  playZap() {
    if (!this.enabled) return;
    this.initCtx();
    if (!this.ctx) return;

    try {
      const bufferSize = this.ctx.sampleRate * 0.08;
      const buffer = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, bufferSize, this.ctx.sampleRate);
      const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
      for (let i = 0; i < bufferSize; i++) {
        data[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.exp(-i / (bufferSize * 0.25));
      }

      const noise = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
      noise.buffer = buffer;

      const filter = this.ctx.createBiquadFilter();
      filter.type = 'bandpass';
      filter.frequency.setValueAtTime(3200, this.ctx.currentTime);
      filter.Q.setValueAtTime(3, this.ctx.currentTime);

      const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12, this.ctx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.08);

      noise.connect(filter);
      filter.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(this.ctx.destination);

      noise.start();
    } catch {
      // AudioContext policy fallback
    }
  }

  playUnlock() {
    if (!this.enabled) return;
    this.initCtx();
    if (!this.ctx) return;

    try {
      const notes = [440, 554.37, 659.25, 880]; // A major cyber chime
      notes.forEach((freq, idx) => {
        if (!this.ctx) return;
        const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
        const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
        const startTime = this.ctx.currentTime + idx * 0.07;

        osc.type = 'sine';
        osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, startTime);

        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, startTime);
        gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.1, startTime + 0.02);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, startTime + 0.35);

        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(this.ctx.destination);

        osc.start(startTime);
        osc.stop(startTime + 0.35);
      });
    } catch {
      // AudioContext policy fallback
    }
  }

  playError() {
    if (!this.enabled) return;
    this.initCtx();
    if (!this.ctx) return;

    try {
      const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
      const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
      osc.type = 'sawtooth';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(140, this.ctx.currentTime);
      osc.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(80, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.18);

      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12, this.ctx.currentTime);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, this.ctx.currentTime + 0.18);

      osc.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(this.ctx.destination);

      osc.start();
      osc.stop(this.ctx.currentTime + 0.18);
    } catch {
      // AudioContext policy fallback
    }
  }

  playVictory() {
    if (!this.enabled) return;
    this.initCtx();
    if (!this.ctx) return;

    try {
      // Grand arpeggio fanfare
      const melody = [523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 1046.50, 1318.51, 1567.98];
      melody.forEach((freq, i) => {
        if (!this.ctx) return;
        const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
        const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
        const start = this.ctx.currentTime + i * 0.09;

        osc.type = 'triangle';
        osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, start);

        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, start);
        gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.15, start + 0.03);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, start + 0.5);

        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(this.ctx.destination);

        osc.start(start);
        osc.stop(start + 0.5);
      });
    } catch {
      // AudioContext policy fallback
    }
  }
}

export const soundFx = new SoundManager();

  animation: shake 0.35s ease;
}

  scrollbar-width: none;
}
.scrollbar-none::-webkit-scrollbar {

=== FILE: ./src/components/FinalProblemView.jsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { FINAL_PROBLEM_STATEMENT } from '../data/puzzles';
import { soundFx } from '../utils/sound';
import { 
  Key, 
  Lock, 
  Download, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  Send, 
  FileCode, 
  ShieldCheck, 
  Copy, 
  Check 
} from 'lucide-react';

export const FinalProblemView = ({
  puzzles,
  activeTeam,
  onJumpToStage,
  onOpenAuth
}) => {
  const [copiedCode, setCopiedCode] = useState(false);
  const [repoUrl, setRepoUrl] = useState('');
  const [timingSlack, setTimingSlack] = useState('');
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState('');
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);

  const solvedIds = activeTeam ? activeTeam.solvedPuzzles : [];
  const solvedCount = solvedIds.length;
  const isFullyUnlocked = solvedCount >= 10;
  const progressPercent = Math.round((solvedCount / 10) * 100);

  const starterVerilogCode = `\`timescale 1ns / 1ps
//////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// Company: ACM Vidyut x Engineer '26 NITK & Arcesium
// Engineer: ${activeTeam ? activeTeam.name : 'Team Unknown'}
// Create Date: 25.10.2026
// Design Name: PROJECT NEURA-CORE
// Module Name: neura_core_top
// Target Devices: Xilinx Artix-7 (xc7a100tcsg324-2)
// Tool Versions: Vivado 2024.1+
// Description: Zero-Latency FPGA Triangular Arbitrage Co-Processor
//////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

module neura_core_top (
    // Primary System Clocks
    input  wire        clk_250m,          // 250 MHz Core Processing Clock
    input  wire        clk_156m_phy,      // 156.25 MHz Ethernet PHY Clock
    input  wire        rst_n,             // Synchronous Active-Low Reset

    // AXI4-Stream Ingress Market Data (64-bit @ 156.25 MHz)
    input  wire [63:0] s_axis_tdata,
    input  wire [7:0]  s_axis_tkeep,
    input  wire        s_axis_tvalid,
    input  wire        s_axis_tlast,
    output wire        s_axis_tready,

    // Market Telemetry Outputs
    output wire [31:0] best_bid_price,
    output wire [31:0] best_ask_price,
    output wire [15:0] tick_sequence_id,

    // Arbitrage Execution Trigger Interface (Zero-Latency)
    output reg         order_fire,
    output reg  [63:0] order_token,
    output reg  [31:0] computed_profit_q16,

    // Status & Diagnostics
    output wire        crc32_pass,
    output wire [11:0] ring_buffer_depth,
    output wire        thermal_alarm
);

    // =========================================================================
    // 1. ASYNCHRONOUS CDC DUAL-CLOCK FIFO (PHY -> CORE)
    // =========================================================================
    // Pointer synchronization via Gray code to prevent multi-bit transit races
    // Gray Formula: G = B ^ (B >> 1)

    // =========================================================================
    // 2. ETHERNET & UDP PACKET HEADER DE-SERIALIZER
    // =========================================================================

    // =========================================================================
    // 3. 16-LEVEL L3 ORDER BOOK PARALLEL COMPARATOR ENGINE
    // =========================================================================

    // =========================================================================
    // 4. Q16.16 FIXED-POINT DSP48E1 ARBITRAGE ARITHMETIC UNIT
    //    Delta = (Bid_A * Bid_B) - Ask_C
    // =========================================================================

    // =========================================================================
    // 5. HARDWARE CRC32 INTEGRITY VERIFICATION (IEEE 802.3)
    // =========================================================================

endmodule`;

  const handleCopyStarter = () => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(starterVerilogCode);
    setCopiedCode(true);
    soundFx.playClick();
    setTimeout(() => setCopiedCode(false), 2000);
  };

  const handleDownloadStarter = () => {
    soundFx.playZap();
    const blob = new Blob([starterVerilogCode], { type: 'text/plain' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = 'neura_core_top.v';
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  const handleSubmitFinal = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!activeTeam) {
      onOpenAuth();
      return;
    }
    soundFx.playVictory();
    setSubmitted(true);
  };

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 py-8 space-y-8">
      {/* Header Banner */}
      <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-[#141518] via-[#121316] to-[#0e0f11] border border-[#282a30] p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(0,240,255,0.15)]">
        <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-start md:items-center justify-between gap-6">
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-mono font-bold bg-cyan-500/20 text-[#00e5ff] border border-cyan-400/30">
                ACM VIDYUT &times; ENGINEER &apos;26
              </span>
              <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-mono font-bold bg-amber-400/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-400/30">
                GRAND HACKATHON MISSION
              </span>
              <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded text-xs font-mono text-[#00e5ff] bg-cyan-950/80 border border-cyan-800">
                SPONSORED BY ARCESIUM
              </span>
            </div>

            <h2 className="text-2xl sm:text-4xl font-black text-white tracking-tight">
              {FINAL_PROBLEM_STATEMENT.title}
            </h2>
            <p className="text-sm sm:text-base text-slate-300 max-w-3xl leading-relaxed">
              {FINAL_PROBLEM_STATEMENT.overview}
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Decryption Gauge Meter */}
          <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-[#0f1012]/80 border border-[#222429] text-center min-w-[200px] shrink-0">
            <div className="text-[11px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
              BLUEPRINT REVEALED
            </div>
            <div className="text-3xl font-black font-mono text-[#00e5ff] my-1">
              {progressPercent}%
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-slate-300 font-medium">
              {solvedCount} of 10 Fragments Solved
            </div>
            <div className="w-full h-2 bg-slate-900 rounded-full mt-2 overflow-hidden border border-slate-700">
              <div 
                className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-400 to-amber-400 transition-all duration-500"
                style={{ width: `${progressPercent}%` }}
              ></div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Target Specs Summary Bar */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-800">
          <div className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 uppercase">Target Device</div>
          <div className="text-sm font-bold text-white font-mono mt-0.5">{FINAL_PROBLEM_STATEMENT.targetHardware}</div>
        </div>
        <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-800">
          <div className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 uppercase">Target Clock Period</div>
          <div className="text-sm font-bold text-[#00e5ff] font-mono mt-0.5">{FINAL_PROBLEM_STATEMENT.clockConstraint}</div>
        </div>
        <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-800">
          <div className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 uppercase">Prize Bounty</div>
          <div className="text-sm font-bold text-amber-400 font-mono mt-0.5">{FINAL_PROBLEM_STATEMENT.prizePool}</div>
        </div>
        <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-800">
          <div className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 uppercase">Title Sponsor</div>
          <div className="text-sm font-bold text-[#00e5ff] font-mono mt-0.5">Arcesium Tech</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 10 Statement Fragments Breakdown Matrix */}
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
            <Key className="w-4 h-4 text-[#00e5ff]" />
            10-Fragment Decryption Netlist
          </h3>
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono">
            Requires solving Puzzles 1 through 10
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          {puzzles.map((p) => {
            const isUnlocked = solvedIds.includes(p.id);

            return (
              <div 
                key={p.id}
                className={`p-4 rounded-xl border transition-all ${
                  isUnlocked
                    ? 'bg-[#141518]/80 border-[#282a30] shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(0,240,255,0.1)]'
                    : 'bg-slate-950/60 border-slate-800/80 opacity-70'
                }`}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-slate-800 pb-2 mb-2.5">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className={`w-5 h-5 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-mono font-bold ${
                      isUnlocked 
                        ? 'bg-cyan-500 text-slate-950 shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(0,240,255,0.5)]' 
                        : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-500'
                    }`}>
                      {p.stageNumber}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-slate-200">
                      {p.fragment.subsystemTag}: {p.fragment.title}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  {isUnlocked ? (
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-[#00e5ff] bg-cyan-950 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-cyan-800">
                      DECRYPTED
                    </span>
                  ) : (
                    <button
                      onClick={() => {
                        soundFx.playClick();
                        onJumpToStage(p.id);
                      }}
                      className="text-[10px] font-mono text-amber-400 hover:text-amber-300 hover:underline flex items-center gap-1"
                    >
                      <Lock className="w-3 h-3" />
                      Solve P{p.stageNumber}
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>

                {isUnlocked ? (
                  <div className="space-y-2 text-xs">
                    <p className="text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">
                      {p.fragment.content}
                    </p>
                    {p.fragment.verilogSignature && (
                      <div className="p-2 rounded bg-black/60 border border-[#1f2125] text-[#00e5ff] font-mono text-[11px] overflow-x-auto">
                        <code>{p.fragment.verilogSignature}</code>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="p-4 rounded bg-slate-900/60 border border-dashed border-slate-800 text-center text-xs text-slate-500 font-mono">
                    [ENCRYPTED BITSTREAM BLOCK &bull; REQUIRES STAGE {p.stageNumber} KEY]
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Master Specification & Starter Kit */}
      <div className={`p-6 sm:p-8 rounded-2xl border transition-all ${
        isFullyUnlocked 
          ? 'bg-[#151619] border-cyan-400 shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(0,240,255,0.25)]' 
          : 'bg-[#111215] border-slate-800'
      }`}>
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-start md:items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-800/80 pb-5 mb-6">
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              {isFullyUnlocked ? (
                <span className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-ping"></span>
              ) : (
                <Lock className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
              )}
              <h3 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-white tracking-tight">
                {isFullyUnlocked ? "Grand Problem Portal Unlocked!" : "Master Problem Specifications & Verilog Starter Kit"}
              </h3>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-300 mt-1">
              {isFullyUnlocked 
                ? "Congratulations! Your team decrypted all 10 hardware fragments. Download the starter repo and submit your synthesized netlist below."
                : `Solve all 10 stages to unlock the full final submission portal. (${10 - solvedCount} remaining)`}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              onClick={handleCopyStarter}
              className="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 text-xs font-mono font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition"
            >
              {copiedCode ? <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" /> : <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
              <span>{copiedCode ? "Copied" : "Copy RTL"}</span>
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={handleDownloadStarter}
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-400 text-slate-950 text-xs font-mono font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(0,240,255,0.3)]"
            >
              <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Download neura_core_top.v</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Requirements & Rubric Grid */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6 mb-6">
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <h4 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[#00e5ff] uppercase tracking-wider">
              CORE HARDWARE ARCHITECTURE CRITERIA
            </h4>
            <ul className="space-y-2 text-xs text-slate-300">
              {FINAL_PROBLEM_STATEMENT.designRequirements.map((req, idx) => (
                <li key={idx} className="flex items-start gap-2">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#00e5ff] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                  <span>{req}</span>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-3">
            <h4 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-amber-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
              REQUIRED DELIVERABLES &amp; EVALUATION RUBRIC
            </h4>
            <ul className="space-y-2 text-xs text-slate-300">
              {FINAL_PROBLEM_STATEMENT.deliverables.map((deliv, idx) => (
                <li key={idx} className="flex items-start gap-2">
                  <FileCode className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                  <span>{deliv}</span>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>

            <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-cyan-950/40 border border-cyan-800/40 text-xs text-cyan-200 mt-4">
              <strong className="font-semibold text-[#00e5ff]">Arcesium Industry Benchmarking: </strong>
              Judges will evaluate peak throughput, minimal pipeline bubble latency, and synthesizability on Xilinx Vivado ML Enterprise.
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Verilog Code Preview Box */}
        <div className="rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-slate-700 bg-black/80">
          <div className="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-900 border-b border-slate-800 text-xs font-mono text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-between">
            <span>neura_core_top.v (Synthesizable RTL Skeleton)</span>
            <span className="text-[#00e5ff]">Verilog-2001</span>
          </div>
          <pre className="p-4 text-xs font-mono text-slate-300 overflow-x-auto max-h-64 leading-relaxed">
            <code>{starterVerilogCode}</code>
          </pre>
        </div>

        {/* Submission Form */}
        <div className="mt-8 pt-6 border-t border-slate-800">
          <h4 className="text-sm font-mono font-bold text-white uppercase tracking-wider mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
            <Send className="w-4 h-4 text-[#00e5ff]" />
            Final Problem Statement Submission Portal
          </h4>

          {submitted ? (
            <div className="p-6 rounded-xl bg-emerald-950/50 border border-emerald-500/50 text-center space-y-2">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-10 h-10 text-emerald-400 mx-auto" />
              <h5 className="text-base font-bold text-white">Submission Successfully Received!</h5>
              <p className="text-xs text-emerald-200 max-w-md mx-auto">
                Your RTL artifacts and timing constraints have been submitted to the ACM Vidyut x Arcesium jury queue. Live leaderboard score updated!
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmitFinal} className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-mono text-slate-300 mb-1">
                  GitHub Repository / Artifact ZIP Link *
                </label>
                <input
                  type="url"
                  required
                  value={repoUrl}
                  onChange={(e) => setRepoUrl(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="https://github.com/your-team/neura-core-rtl"
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 text-white text-xs font-mono focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-400"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-mono text-slate-300 mb-1">
                  Reported Worst Negative Slack (WNS at 250 MHz)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={timingSlack}
                  onChange={(e) => setTimingSlack(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="+0.342 ns (Vivado Timing Report)"
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 text-white text-xs font-mono focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-400"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="md:col-span-2">
                <label className="block text-xs font-mono text-slate-300 mb-1">
                  Architectural Notes &amp; Pipeline Latency Breakdown
                </label>
                <textarea
                  rows={2}
                  value={notes}
                  onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Summarize your CDC queue depth, DSP pipeline stages, and testbench validation..."
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 text-white text-xs font-mono focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-400"
                ></textarea>
              </div>

              <div className="md:col-span-2 flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                  {activeTeam ? `Submitting on behalf of ${activeTeam.name}` : "Please sign in to submit"}
                </span>

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="px-6 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-400 to-blue-500 hover:from-cyan-300 hover:to-blue-400 text-slate-950 font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-mono shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(0,240,255,0.4)] flex items-center gap-2 transition"
                >
                  <Send className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Transmit Final Artifacts</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

=== FILE: ./src/components/LiveAnalysisView.jsx ===
import React from 'react';
import { 
  BarChart3, 
  Activity, 
  AlertOctagon,
  Timer,
  TrendingUp
} from 'lucide-react';

export const LiveAnalysisView = ({
  teams,
  puzzles,
  activeTeam
}) => {
  const totalTeams = teams.length;

  // Compute analytics per puzzle
  const puzzleStats = puzzles.map((puzzle) => {
    let solvesCount = 0;
    let fastestSolveDurationMs = Infinity;
    let fastestTeamName = 'None';
    let totalSolveDurationMs = 0;

    teams.forEach((t) => {
      if (t.solvedPuzzles.includes(puzzle.id)) {
        solvesCount++;
        const ts = t.puzzleSolveTimestamps[puzzle.id];
        // Calculate solve time relative to previous puzzle solve or start
        const prevTs = puzzle.id === 1 ? ts - 15 * 60 * 1000 : (t.puzzleSolveTimestamps[puzzle.id - 1] || ts - 20 * 60 * 1000);
        const duration = Math.max(ts - prevTs, 3 * 60 * 1000); // minimum 3 mins

        totalSolveDurationMs += duration;
        if (duration < fastestSolveDurationMs) {
          fastestSolveDurationMs = duration;
          fastestTeamName = t.name;
        }
      }
    });

    const completionRate = totalTeams > 0 ? Math.round((solvesCount / totalTeams) * 100) : 0;
    const avgDurationMins = solvesCount > 0 ? Math.round(totalSolveDurationMs / solvesCount / (60 * 1000)) : 0;
    const fastestMins = fastestSolveDurationMs !== Infinity ? Math.round(fastestSolveDurationMs / (60 * 1000)) : 0;

    return {
      puzzle,
      solvesCount,
      completionRate,
      fastestTeamName,
      fastestMins,
      avgDurationMins,
      isBottleneck: completionRate < 40 && puzzle.id > 4
    };
  });

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 py-8 space-y-8">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="p-6 rounded-2xl bg-[#141518]/90 border border-[#23252a] shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(0,240,255,0.12)]">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-cyan-400 animate-ping"></span>
          <span className="text-xs font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-[#00e5ff] font-bold">
            STATISTICAL TELEMETRY
          </span>
          <span className="text-slate-500">&bull;</span>
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">Live Task Completion Analysis</span>
        </div>
        <h2 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-black text-white tracking-tight mt-1 flex items-center gap-2">
          <BarChart3 className="w-7 h-7 text-[#00e5ff]" />
          RTL Puzzle Velocity &amp; Bottleneck Analysis
        </h2>
        <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-300 mt-1">
          Detailed metrics by task: completion velocity, fastest solves, stage clearance friction, and team timeline trajectories.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Metric KPI cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/70 border border-slate-800">
          <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400 uppercase">Active Competing Teams</div>
          <div className="text-2xl font-black text-white font-mono mt-1">{totalTeams} Teams</div>
          <div className="text-[11px] text-emerald-400 mt-1 flex items-center gap-1">
            <Activity className="w-3 h-3" /> 100% telemetry online
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/70 border border-slate-800">
          <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400 uppercase">Total Verified Solves</div>
          <div className="text-2xl font-black text-[#00e5ff] font-mono mt-1">
            {teams.reduce((acc, t) => acc + t.solvedPuzzles.length, 0)} Solves
          </div>
          <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-1">Across 10 hardware stages</div>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/70 border border-slate-800">
          <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400 uppercase">Average Solve Time</div>
          <div className="text-2xl font-black text-amber-400 font-mono mt-1">16.4 mins</div>
          <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-1">Per unlocked hardware stage</div>
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/70 border border-slate-800">
          <div className="text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-400 uppercase">Leader Pace</div>
          <div className="text-2xl font-black text-purple-400 font-mono mt-1">9 / 10 Stages</div>
          <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-1">RTL_Overclockers (NITK)</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Stage-by-Stage Completion Breakdown Table */}
      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-[#131417]/90 overflow-hidden shadow-2xl">
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/80 flex items-center justify-between">
          <h3 className="text-sm font-mono font-bold text-white uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
            <Timer className="w-4 h-4 text-[#00e5ff]" />
            Task Completion &amp; Speed Metrics (P1 &ndash; P10)
          </h3>
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono">Updated in Real Time</span>
        </div>

        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left text-xs">
            <thead className="bg-[#0f1012] text-slate-400 font-mono text-[11px] uppercase border-b border-slate-800">
              <tr>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">Stage #</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4">Challenge Name</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-3">Category</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-3 text-center">Completion Rate</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-3 text-center">Fastest Solve</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-3 text-center">Avg Time</th>
                <th className="py-3 px-4 text-right">Difficulty Status</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-800/60 font-sans">
              {puzzleStats.map(({ puzzle, solvesCount, completionRate, fastestTeamName, fastestMins, avgDurationMins, isBottleneck }) => (
                <tr key={puzzle.id} className="hover:bg-slate-900/40 transition">
                  <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-mono font-bold text-[#00e5ff]">
                    STAGE {puzzle.stageNumber}
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-semibold text-white">
                    {puzzle.title}
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3.5 px-3">
                    <span className="font-mono text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-800 text-slate-300">
                      {puzzle.category}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3.5 px-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 justify-center">
                      <div className="w-20 h-2 bg-slate-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                        <div 
                          className="h-full bg-cyan-400 rounded-full"
                          style={{ width: `${completionRate}%` }}
                        ></div>
                      </div>
                      <span className="font-mono font-bold text-slate-200 text-[11px]">
                        {completionRate}% ({solvesCount}/{totalTeams})
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3.5 px-3 text-center font-mono">
                    {fastestMins > 0 ? (
                      <span className="text-emerald-400 font-semibold">
                        {fastestMins}m <span className="text-slate-500 font-sans text-[10px]">({fastestTeamName})</span>
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="text-slate-600">—</span>
                    )}
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3.5 px-3 text-center font-mono text-slate-300">
                    {avgDurationMins > 0 ? `${avgDurationMins} mins` : '—'}
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-right">
                    {isBottleneck ? (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-rose-950 text-rose-300 border border-rose-800 text-[10px] font-mono font-bold">
                        <AlertOctagon className="w-3 h-3" /> BOTTLENECK
                      </span>
                    ) : completionRate >= 70 ? (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-800 text-[10px] font-mono font-bold">
                        FAST CLEAR
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-sky-950 text-sky-300 border border-sky-800 text-[10px] font-mono font-bold">
                        BALANCED
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Team Trajectory Timeline */}
      <div className="p-6 rounded-2xl bg-[#121316]/90 border border-slate-800 space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <h3 className="text-sm font-mono font-bold text-white uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-2">
            <TrendingUp className="w-4 h-4 text-[#00e5ff]" />
            Live Stage Clearance Timeline Across Teams
          </h3>
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-mono">X-Axis: Stages Solved (0 to 10)</span>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-3 pt-2">
          {teams.map((team) => {
            const count = team.solvedPuzzles.length;
            const percent = (count / 10) * 100;
            const isUser = activeTeam?.id === team.id;

            return (
              <div 
                key={team.id}
                className={`p-3 rounded-xl border transition ${
                  isUser ? 'bg-cyan-950/30 border-cyan-400/50' : 'bg-slate-900/50 border-slate-800/80'

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { soundFx } from '../utils/sound';
import { Cpu, Activity, Zap, Shield, Sparkles, Layers } from 'lucide-react';

export const InteractiveHoloCore = () => {
  const [rotate, setRotate] = useState({ x: 10, y: -15 });
  const [clockMhz, setClockMhz] = useState(250);
  const [activeLayer, setActiveLayer] = useState('ALL');
  const [isInspecting, setIsInspecting] = useState(false);
  const containerRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleMouseMove = (e) => {
      if (!containerRef.current) return;
      const rect = containerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
      const centerX = rect.left + rect.width / 2;
      const centerY = rect.top + rect.height / 2;

      // Mouse distance from center
      const dx = (e.clientX - centerX) / (window.innerWidth / 2);
      const dy = (e.clientY - centerY) / (window.innerHeight / 2);

      // Smooth tilt angles
      const rotY = Math.max(-25, Math.min(25, dx * 28));
      const rotX = Math.max(-25, Math.min(25, -dy * 28));

      setRotate({ x: rotX, y: rotY });
    };

    window.addEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
  }, []);

  const handleOverclock = () => {
    soundFx.playZap();
    setClockMhz((prev) => (prev === 250 ? 350 : prev === 350 ? 400 : 250));
  };

  return (
    <div
      ref={containerRef}
      className="relative flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 rounded-3xl metamask-card spotlight-card overflow-hidden group select-none"
    >
      {/* Ambient background glow orb */}
      <div className="absolute -top-16 -right-16 w-64 h-64 bg-cyan-500/15 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none"></div>
      <div className="absolute -bottom-16 -left-16 w-64 h-64 bg-purple-600/15 rounded-full blur-3xl pointer-events-none"></div>

      {/* Top telemetry status line */}
      <div className="w-full flex items-center justify-between border-b border-cyan-500/20 pb-3 mb-4 text-xs font-mono">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-cyan-400 animate-ping"></span>
          <span className="text-cyan-300 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
            3D SILICON DIE ARCHITECTURE
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={handleOverclock}
            className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-cyan-950/80 border border-cyan-500/40 text-cyan-300 font-mono text-[10px] hover:bg-cyan-900 transition flex items-center gap-1"
            title="Click to toggle clock frequency"
          >
            <Activity className="w-3 h-3 text-cyan-400" />
            <span>F_CLK: {clockMhz} MHz</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 3D Holographic Silicon Processor Container */}
      <div className="w-64 h-64 sm:w-72 sm:h-72 my-2 perspective-1000 flex items-center justify-center">
        <div
          className="relative w-48 h-48 sm:w-56 sm:h-56 transition-transform duration-200 ease-out preserve-3d"
          style={{
            transform: `rotateX(${rotate.x}deg) rotateY(${rotate.y}deg)`,
          }}
        >
          {/* Layer 1: Base Substrate BGA PCB (Dark Carbon & Gold Pins) */}
          <div 
            className="absolute inset-0 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-[#0c1c38] via-[#09152b] to-[#040915] border-2 border-cyan-400/40 shadow-[0_20px_50px_rgba(0,0,0,0.8),0_0_30px_rgba(0,240,255,0.25)] flex items-center justify-center"
            style={{ transform: 'translateZ(0px)' }}
          >
            {/* Corner alignment markers */}
            <div className="absolute top-2 left-2 w-2.5 h-2.5 border-t-2 border-l-2 border-amber-400"></div>
            <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 w-2.5 h-2.5 border-t-2 border-r-2 border-amber-400"></div>
            <div className="absolute bottom-2 left-2 w-2.5 h-2.5 border-b-2 border-l-2 border-amber-400"></div>
            <div className="absolute bottom-2 right-2 w-2.5 h-2.5 border-b-2 border-r-2 border-amber-400"></div>

            {/* Perimeter BGA Pins */}
            <div className="absolute inset-2 border border-dashed border-cyan-500/30 rounded-xl"></div>
          </div>

          {/* Layer 2: Intermediate Circuit Waveform Layer */}
          <div

      {/* Interactive Micro-Inspectors */}

=== FILE: ./src/components/HeroBanner.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { soundFx } from '../utils/sound';
import {
  ArrowRight,
  Trophy,
  Eye,
  Calendar,
  MapPin,
  Sparkles,
  QrCode,
  CheckCircle2,
  ExternalLink
} from 'lucide-react';

export const HeroBanner = ({
  activeTeam,
  solvedCount,
  totalPuzzles,
  onSelectTab,
  onOpenAuthModal,
  onJumpToCurrentPuzzle
}) => {
  const [showPosterModal, setShowPosterModal] = useState(false);
  const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState({ days: 0, hours: 0, minutes: 0, seconds: 0 });

  useEffect(() => {
    const target = new Date('2026-10-25T09:00:00+05:30').getTime();
    const tick = () => {
      const diff = target - Date.now();
      if (diff > 0) {
        setTimeLeft({
          days:    Math.floor(diff / 86400000),
          hours:   Math.floor((diff / 3600000) % 24),
          minutes: Math.floor((diff / 60000) % 60),
          seconds: Math.floor((diff / 1000) % 60),
        });
      }
    };
    tick();
    const id = setInterval(tick, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, []);

  const progress = Math.round((solvedCount / totalPuzzles) * 100);

  return (

=== FILE: ./src/components/ElectricCanvas.jsx ===
import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

export const ElectricCanvas = () => {
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return;

    let animationFrameId;
    let width = (canvas.width = window.innerWidth);
    let height = (canvas.height = window.innerHeight);

    const handleResize = () => {
      if (!canvas) return;
      width = canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
      height = canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
      initNodes();
    };

    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);

    const mouse = { x: -1000, y: -1000, active: false };

    const handleMouseMove = (e) => {
      mouse.x = e.clientX;
      mouse.y = e.clientY;
      mouse.active = true;
    };

    const handleMouseLeave = () => {
      mouse.active = false;
    };

    window.addEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
    window.addEventListener('mouseleave', handleMouseLeave);

    let nodes = [];
    const initNodes = () => {
      nodes = [];
      const cols = Math.floor(width / 170) + 1;
      const rows = Math.floor(height / 170) + 1;

      for (let i = 0; i < cols; i++) {
        for (let j = 0; j < rows; j++) {
          nodes.push({
            x: i * 170 + Math.sin(j * 3 + i) * 25,
            y: j * 170 + Math.cos(i * 2 + j) * 25,
            connections: [],
            pulse: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2
          });
        }
      }

      for (let i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
        for (let j = i + 1; j < nodes.length; j++) {
          const dx = nodes[i].x - nodes[j].x;
          const dy = nodes[i].y - nodes[j].y;
          const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
          if (dist < 180 && nodes[i].connections.length < 3) {
            nodes[i].connections.push(j);
          }
        }
      }
    };

    initNodes();

    // Floating subtle sparks in dark matte space
    const particles = [];
    const particleCount = 28;
    const colors = ['#00e5ff', '#ffffff', '#fbbf24', '#9ca3af'];

    for (let i = 0; i < particleCount; i++) {
      particles.push({
        x: Math.random() * width,
        y: Math.random() * height,
        vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4,
        vy: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4,
        size: Math.random() * 1.5 + 0.8,
        alpha: Math.random() * 0.35 + 0.1,
        color: colors[Math.floor(Math.random() * colors.length)]
      });
    }

    let tick = 0;

    const render = () => {
      tick += 0.015;
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

      // Matte circuit traces: neutral charcoal lines, not neon blue
      ctx.lineWidth = 1;
      for (let i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
        const node = nodes[i];
        node.pulse += 0.015;

        for (const targetIdx of node.connections) {
          const target = nodes[targetIdx];
          const distToMouse = mouse.active
            ? Math.hypot((node.x + target.x) / 2 - mouse.x, (node.y + target.y) / 2 - mouse.y)
            : 9999;

          const isNearMouse = distToMouse < 160;

          ctx.strokeStyle = isNearMouse ? 'rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.45)' : 'rgba(38, 41, 46, 0.5)';
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(node.x, node.y);
          ctx.lineTo(target.x, target.y);
          ctx.stroke();

          // Sporadic spark
          const pulseOffset = (Math.sin(node.pulse + i) + 1) / 2;
          const px = node.x + (target.x - node.x) * pulseOffset;
          const py = node.y + (target.y - node.y) * pulseOffset;

          if (Math.sin(tick * 1.8 + i) > 0.75) {
            ctx.fillStyle = isNearMouse ? '#00e5ff' : 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.25)';
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.arc(px, py, 1.4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            ctx.fill();
          }
        }

        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(45, 48, 54, 0.6)';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(node.x, node.y, 1.6, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }

      // Mouse interactive circuit reaction (crisp, subtle)
      if (mouse.active) {
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0, 229, 255, 0.5)';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1.1;

        for (const node of nodes) {
          const dist = Math.hypot(node.x - mouse.x, node.y - mouse.y);
          if (dist > 30 && dist < 120) {
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(node.x, node.y);
            const midX = (node.x + mouse.x) / 2 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 8;
            const midY = (node.y + mouse.y) / 2 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 8;
            ctx.lineTo(midX, midY);
            ctx.lineTo(mouse.x, mouse.y);
            ctx.stroke();
          }
        }
      }

      // Particles
      for (const p of particles) {
        p.x += p.vx;
        p.y += p.vy;

        if (p.x < 0) p.x = width;
        if (p.x > width) p.x = 0;
        if (p.y < 0) p.y = height;
        if (p.y > height) p.y = 0;

        ctx.fillStyle = p.color;
        ctx.globalAlpha = p.alpha;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.size, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }

      ctx.globalAlpha = 1.0;
      animationFrameId = requestAnimationFrame(render);
    };

    render();

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);
      window.removeEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
      window.removeEventListener('mouseleave', handleMouseLeave);
      cancelAnimationFrame(animationFrameId);
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <canvas
      ref={canvasRef}
      className="fixed inset-0 pointer-events-none z-0 opacity-60"
      style={{ display: 'block' }}
    />
  );
};


=== FILE: ./src/components/PuzzleStageList.jsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { PuzzleCard } from './PuzzleCard';
import { InteractiveHoloCore } from './InteractiveHoloCore';
import { soundFx } from '../utils/sound';
import { Key, Search, Cpu, ChevronDown, ChevronUp } from 'lucide-react';

export const PuzzleStageList = ({
  puzzles,
  activeTeam,
  onSolve,
  onUseHint,
  usedHints,
  onOpenAuth,
  onNavigateToFinal
}) => {
  const [filterCategory, setFilterCategory] = useState('ALL');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [showHoloInspector, setShowHoloInspector] = useState(false);

  const solvedIds = activeTeam ? activeTeam.solvedPuzzles : [];
  const solvedCount = solvedIds.length;
  const nextPuzzleId = solvedCount + 1;

  const filteredPuzzles = puzzles.filter((p) => {
    const matchesCat = filterCategory === 'ALL' || p.category === filterCategory;
    const matchesQuery =
      searchQuery === '' ||
      p.title.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      p.puzzleStatement.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      String(p.stageNumber).includes(searchQuery);
    return matchesCat && matchesQuery;
  });

  const categories = ['ALL', 'LOGIC GATES', 'VERILOG RTL', 'TIMING & METASTABILITY', 'FSM ARCHITECTURE', 'FPGA HARDWARE'];

  return (
    <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 py-8 space-y-6">

      {/* ── Top Header Banner ── */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-start md:items-center justify-between gap-4 p-5 rounded-2xl bg-[#141517] border border-[#232529]">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
            <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-[#00e5ff]" />
            <span className="text-[11px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 font-bold">
              ACM VIDYUT × ENGINEER NITK '26
            </span>
          </div>
          <h2 className="text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black text-white tracking-tight">
            10 Stage Decryption Matrix
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-zinc-400 mt-1 max-w-xl">
            Each decrypted stage unlocks 10% of the grand FPGA co-processor problem statement. Stages must be solved sequentially.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 shrink-0 w-full md:w-auto justify-end">
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              soundFx.playZap();
              setShowHoloInspector(!showHoloInspector);
            }}
            className="btn-matte-ghost text-xs"
          >
            <Cpu className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#00e5ff]" />
            <span>{showHoloInspector ? 'Hide 3D Core' : '3D Silicon Die'}</span>
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => {
              soundFx.playClick();
              onNavigateToFinal();
            }}
            className="btn-matte-primary text-xs"
          >
            <Key className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Final Blueprint</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── Optional 3D Holo Core ── */}
      {showHoloInspector && (
        <div className="animate-fadeIn">
          <InteractiveHoloCore />
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ── Quick Stage Jumper Strip ── */}
      <div className="matte-card p-4 space-y-2">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 mb-1">
          <span>Jump to stage</span>
          <span className="text-[#00e5ff] font-bold">{solvedCount} / {puzzles.length} Decrypted</span>
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-5 sm:grid-cols-10 gap-1.5">
          {puzzles.map((p) => {
            const isSolved = solvedIds.includes(p.id);
            const isNext = p.id === nextPuzzleId;

            return (
              <a
                key={p.id}
                href={`#stage-${p.id}`}
                onClick={(e) => {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  soundFx.playClick();
                  document.getElementById(`stage-${p.id}`)?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
                }}
                className={`flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-2 px-1 rounded-lg border text-center transition ${
                  isSolved
                    ? 'bg-[#152327] border-[#00e5ff]/40 text-[#00e5ff]'
                    : isNext
                    ? 'bg-[#262115] border-amber-500/50 text-amber-300'
                    : 'bg-[#18191c] border-[#25272c] text-zinc-600 hover:border-[#383b43]'
                }`}
              >
                <span className="text-[11px] font-mono font-bold">P{p.stageNumber}</span>
                <span className="text-[9px] mt-0.5 font-medium">
                  {isSolved ? '✓' : isNext ? 'ACTIVE' : '—'}
                </span>
              </a>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── Category Filters and Search ── */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto w-full sm:w-auto scrollbar-none pb-1 sm:pb-0">
          {categories.map((cat) => (
            <button
              key={cat}
              onClick={() => {
                soundFx.playClick();
                setFilterCategory(cat);
              }}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-mono whitespace-nowrap transition border ${
                filterCategory === cat
                  ? 'bg-[#202329] border-[#373b45] text-white font-bold'
                  : 'bg-[#141517] border-[#222429] text-zinc-400 hover:text-white hover:border-[#30333b]'
              }`}
            >
              {cat}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="relative w-full sm:w-60">
          <Search className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-zinc-500 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
          <input
            type="text"
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search puzzle statement..."
            className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-[#141517] border border-[#25272c] text-xs text-white placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#00e5ff] transition"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── Puzzle Cards ── */}
      <div className="space-y-5">
        {filteredPuzzles.map((puzzle) => {
          const isSolved = solvedIds.includes(puzzle.id);
          const isUnlocked = puzzle.id === 1 || solvedIds.includes(puzzle.id - 1);
          const isNextToSolve = puzzle.id === nextPuzzleId;

          return (
            <PuzzleCard
              key={puzzle.id}
              puzzle={puzzle}
              isUnlocked={isUnlocked}
              isSolved={isSolved}
              isNextToSolve={isNextToSolve}
              onSolve={onSolve}
              onUseHint={onUseHint}
              hasUsedHint={usedHints.includes(puzzle.id)}
              activeTeam={activeTeam}
              onOpenAuth={onOpenAuth}
            />
          );
        })}
      </div>

    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { soundFx } from '../utils/sound';
import {
  Cpu,
  Trophy,
  Key,
  BarChart3,
  FileText,
  Volume2,
  VolumeX,
  User,
  LogOut,
  ExternalLink,
  Menu,
  X
} from 'lucide-react';

export const Navbar = ({
  currentTab,
  onSelectTab,
  activeTeam,
  onOpenAuthModal,
  onLogout,
  soundEnabled,
  onToggleSound,
  solvedCount,
  totalPuzzles
}) => {
  const [mobileMenuOpen, setMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const percentDecrypted = Math.round((solvedCount / totalPuzzles) * 100);

  const NAV_ITEMS = [
    { id: 'puzzles',       label: '10 Puzzles',       icon: Cpu },
    { id: 'final-problem', label: 'Grand Problem',    icon: Key },
    { id: 'leaderboard',   label: 'Live Standings',   icon: Trophy, live: true },
    { id: 'analysis',      label: 'Telemetry',        icon: BarChart3 },
    { id: 'rules',         label: 'Event Rubric',     icon: FileText },
  ];

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 bg-[#0a0b0d]/90 backdrop-blur-md border-b border-[#1a1b1f]">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 h-14 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
        
        {/* Left: Brand Identity */}
        <div
          onClick={() => {
            soundFx.playClick();
            onSelectTab('puzzles');
          }}
          className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer group select-none shrink-0"
        >
          <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-[#141518] border border-[#23252b] flex items-center justify-center text-[#00e5ff] group-hover:border-[#00e5ff]/50 transition">
            <Cpu className="w-4 h-4" />
          </div>
          <div className="leading-tight">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="text-sm font-extrabold text-white tracking-widest font-mono group-hover:text-[#00e5ff] transition">
                VERILOG QUEST
              </span>
              <span className="text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded bg-[#18191d] border border-[#25272e] text-zinc-400 font-mono">
                '26
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500 font-medium">
              ACM Vidyut × Engineer NITK
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Center: Tabs */}
        <nav className="hidden lg:flex items-center gap-1 bg-[#101114] p-1 rounded-xl border border-[#1c1e22]">
          {NAV_ITEMS.map(({ id, label, icon: Icon, live }) => {
            const isActive = currentTab === id;
            return (
              <button
                key={id}
                onClick={() => {
                  soundFx.playClick();
                  onSelectTab(id);
                }}
                className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide transition ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'bg-[#1a1b20] text-white border border-[#2b2d35]'
                    : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-[#141518]'
                }`}
              >
                <Icon className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${isActive ? 'text-[#00e5ff]' : 'text-zinc-500'}`} />
                <span>{label}</span>
                {live && <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse" />}
                {id === 'final-problem' && (
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-amber-400 bg-amber-950/40 px-1 rounded border border-amber-800/40">
                    {percentDecrypted}%
                  </span>
                )}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </nav>

        {/* Right: Team Profile & Audio */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <button
            onClick={onToggleSound}
            aria-label="Toggle sound"
            title={soundEnabled ? 'Sound FX: ON' : 'Sound FX: OFF'}
            className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-lg border flex items-center justify-center transition ${
              soundEnabled
                ? 'bg-[#141518] border-[#25272d] text-zinc-300 hover:text-white'
                : 'bg-[#0f1012] border-[#18191c] text-zinc-600'
            }`}
          >
            {soundEnabled ? <Volume2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#00e5ff]" /> : <VolumeX className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
          </button>

          {activeTeam ? (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-[#121316] border border-[#202227] rounded-xl px-2.5 py-1">
              <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-md bg-[#191a1e] flex items-center justify-center text-xs text-amber-400 font-bold border border-[#282a31]">
                {activeTeam.avatarSeed || '⚡'}
              </div>
              <div className="hidden sm:block text-left">
                <div className="text-xs font-bold text-white max-w-[100px] truncate leading-tight">
                  {activeTeam.name}
                </div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500 font-mono leading-tight">
                  {solvedCount}/10 · {activeTeam.score} pts
                </div>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  soundFx.playClick();
                  onLogout();
                }}
                title="Switch team / Logout"
                className="ml-1 p-1 text-zinc-500 hover:text-rose-400 rounded transition"
              >
                <LogOut className="w-3 h-3" />
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                soundFx.playClick();
                onOpenAuthModal();
              }}
              className="btn-matte-primary !py-1.5 !px-3.5 !text-xs"
            >
              <User className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Login</span>
            </button>
          )}

          <button
            onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(!mobileMenuOpen)}
            className="lg:hidden p-1.5 rounded-lg border border-[#1e2025] text-zinc-400 hover:text-white"
          >
            {mobileMenuOpen ? <X className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Menu className="w-4 h-4" />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Mobile Drawer */}
      {mobileMenuOpen && (
        <div className="lg:hidden border-t border-[#1a1b1f] bg-[#0c0d0f] px-4 py-2 space-y-1">
          {NAV_ITEMS.map(({ id, label, icon: Icon, live }) => (
            <button
              key={id}
              onClick={() => {
                soundFx.playClick();
                onSelectTab(id);
                setMobileMenuOpen(false);
              }}
              className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold ${
                currentTab === id
                  ? 'bg-[#18191e] text-white border border-[#282a32]'
                  : 'text-zinc-400 hover:bg-[#121316]'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Icon className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>{label}</span>
              </div>
              {live && <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse" />}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </header>
  );
};


=== FILE: ./src/components/LeaderboardView.jsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { soundFx } from '../utils/sound';
import { Trophy, Search, Filter, Radio, Play, Pause } from 'lucide-react';

export const LeaderboardView = ({
  teams,
  puzzles,
  activeTeam,
  activityLogs,
  isSimulating,
  onToggleSimulation
}) => {
  const [searchTeam, setSearchTeam] = useState('');
  const [selectedCollege, setSelectedCollege] = useState('ALL');

  const sortedTeams = [...teams].sort((a, b) => {
    if (b.solvedPuzzles.length !== a.solvedPuzzles.length) return b.solvedPuzzles.length - a.solvedPuzzles.length;
    if (b.score !== a.score) return b.score - a.score;
    return a.penaltyMinutes - b.penaltyMinutes;
  });

  const colleges = ['ALL', ...Array.from(new Set(teams.map((t) => t.college)))];

  const filteredTeams = sortedTeams.filter((t) => {
    const matchesSearch =
      t.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchTeam.toLowerCase()) ||
      t.college.toLowerCase().includes(searchTeam.toLowerCase()) ||
      t.members.some((m) => m.toLowerCase().includes(searchTeam.toLowerCase()));
    const matchesCollege = selectedCollege === 'ALL' || t.college === selectedCollege;
    return matchesSearch && matchesCollege;
  });

  const formatTimeAgo = (ts) => {
    if (!ts) return '—';
    const d = Math.max(0, Date.now() - ts);
    const m = Math.floor(d / 60000);
    if (m < 1) return 'Just now';
    if (m < 60) return `${m}m ago`;
    return `${Math.floor(m / 60)}h ${m % 60}m ago`;
  };

  const MEDAL = ['🥇', '🥈', '🥉'];

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 py-8 space-y-8">
      
      {/* ── Top Header Bar ── */}
      <div className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-[#141517] border border-[#232529] flex flex-col md:flex-row items-start md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
            <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-400 animate-pulse" />
            <span className="text-xs font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-400 font-bold">
              LIVE BROADCAST &bull; VERILOG QUEST '26
            </span>
            <span className="text-zinc-600">&bull;</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-zinc-400">ACM Vidyut × Engineer NITK '26</span>
          </div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-white tracking-tight flex items-center gap-2">
            <Trophy className="w-6 h-6 text-amber-400" />
            Official Standings Matrix
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-zinc-400 mt-1">
            Real-time stage completion timestamps, cumulative scores, and timing penalties across all 10 stages.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => {
            soundFx.playClick();
            onToggleSimulation();
          }}
          className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-xl border text-xs font-mono font-bold flex items-center gap-2 transition ${
            isSimulating
              ? 'bg-[#15231c] border-emerald-500/50 text-emerald-300'
              : 'bg-[#18191c] border-[#2b2d35] text-zinc-300 hover:border-zinc-500'
          }`}
        >
          {isSimulating ? (
            <>
              <Pause className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
              <span>Simulation Streaming</span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <Play className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#00e5ff]" />
              <span>Simulate Live Event</span>
            </>
          )}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* ── Podium Cards for Top 3 ── */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        {sortedTeams.slice(0, 3).map((team, idx) => (
          <div
            key={team.id}
            className={`p-5 rounded-xl border matte-card relative overflow-hidden ${
              idx === 0
                ? '!border-amber-500/40 !bg-[#1c1a14]'
                : idx === 1
                ? '!border-zinc-500/30 !bg-[#18191d]'
                : '!border-amber-800/30 !bg-[#191614]'
            }`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
              <span className="text-2xl">{MEDAL[idx]}</span>
              <span className="text-2xl">{team.avatarSeed}</span>
            </div>
            <h4 className="text-base font-extrabold text-white truncate">{team.name}</h4>
            <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 truncate mb-4">{team.college}</p>
            <div className="pt-3 border-t border-[#23252a] flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-mono">
              <span className="text-zinc-400">Solved: <strong className="text-[#00e5ff]">{team.solvedPuzzles.length}/10</strong></span>
              <span className="text-amber-400 font-bold">{team.score.toLocaleString()} pts</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* ── Filter & Search Bar ── */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto w-full sm:w-auto scrollbar-none">
          <span className="text-xs font-mono text-zinc-500 mr-1 flex items-center gap-1">
            <Filter className="w-3 h-3" /> Institution:
          </span>
          {colleges.map((c) => (
            <button
              key={c}
              onClick={() => {
                soundFx.playClick();
                setSelectedCollege(c);
              }}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-mono whitespace-nowrap transition border ${
                selectedCollege === c
                  ? 'bg-[#202228] border-[#373b44] text-white font-bold'
                  : 'bg-[#141517] border-[#222429] text-zinc-400 hover:text-white'
              }`}
            >
              {c}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="relative w-full sm:w-60">
          <Search className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-zinc-500 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
          <input
            type="text"
            value={searchTeam}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchTeam(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search team or member..."
            className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-[#141517] border border-[#232529] text-xs text-white placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#00e5ff] transition"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── Standings Table ── */}
      <div className="matte-card overflow-hidden">
        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-left text-xs">
            <thead className="bg-[#101113] border-b border-[#202227] text-zinc-400 font-mono text-[11px] uppercase">
              <tr>
                <th className="py-3.5 px-4 w-16 text-center">Rank</th>
                <th className="py-3.5 px-4">Team &bull; Institution</th>
                <th className="py-3.5 px-3 text-center hidden md:table-cell">Stages (P1 &ndash; P10)</th>
                <th className="py-3.5 px-3 text-center hidden sm:table-cell">Last Solve</th>
                <th className="py-3.5 px-3 text-right hidden sm:table-cell">Penalty</th>
                <th className="py-3.5 px-4 text-right">Score</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#1e2025] font-sans">
              {filteredTeams.map((team, idx) => {
                const isCurrentUserTeam = activeTeam?.id === team.id;
                const latestSolveTime = Math.max(...Object.values(team.puzzleSolveTimestamps), 0);

                return (
                  <tr
                    key={team.id}
                    className={`transition-colors ${
                      isCurrentUserTeam
                        ? 'bg-[#131b1d] ring-1 ring-inset ring-[#00e5ff]/25'
                        : 'hover:bg-[#16171a]'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <td className="py-4 px-4 text-center font-mono font-bold text-sm">
                      {idx < 3 ? MEDAL[idx] : <span className="text-zinc-500">#{idx + 1}</span>}
                    </td>

                    <td className="py-4 px-4">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                        <span className="text-lg">{team.avatarSeed}</span>
                        <div>
                          <div className="font-bold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
                            <span>{team.name}</span>
                            {isCurrentUserTeam && (
                              <span className="px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded text-[10px] bg-[#00e5ff] text-[#0c0d0e] font-bold font-mono">
                                YOU
                              </span>
                            )}
                          </div>
                          <div className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 mt-0.5">
                            {team.college} &bull; {team.members.join(', ')}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </td>

                    <td className="py-4 px-3 text-center hidden md:table-cell">
                      <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                        {puzzles.map((p) => {
                          const solved = team.solvedPuzzles.includes(p.id);
                          return (
                            <div
                              key={p.id}
                              title={`P${p.stageNumber}: ${p.title} - ${solved ? 'Solved' : 'Pending'}`}
                              className={`w-5 h-5 rounded flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-mono font-bold border ${
                                solved
                                  ? 'bg-[#142327] border-[#00e5ff]/40 text-[#00e5ff]'
                                  : 'bg-[#18191c] border-[#222429] text-zinc-700'
                              }`}
                            >
                              {solved ? '✓' : p.stageNumber}
                            </div>
                          );
                        })}
                      </div>
                    </td>

                    <td className="py-4 px-3 text-center font-mono text-zinc-400 hidden sm:table-cell">
                      {formatTimeAgo(latestSolveTime)}
                    </td>

                    <td className="py-4 px-3 text-right font-mono text-amber-400/90 hidden sm:table-cell">
                      {team.penaltyMinutes > 0 ? `+${team.penaltyMinutes}m` : '0m'}
                    </td>

                    <td className="py-4 px-4 text-right font-mono font-bold text-sm text-white">
                      {team.score.toLocaleString()}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── Event Activity Log ── */}
      <div className="matte-card p-5">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Radio className="w-4 h-4 text-[#00e5ff] animate-pulse" />
            <h3 className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-white uppercase tracking-wider">
              Live Competition Activity Log
            </h3>
          </div>
          <span className="text-[11px] font-mono text-zinc-500">
            {activityLogs.length} events recorded
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-1.5 max-h-56 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
          {activityLogs.map((act) => (
            <div
              key={act.id}
              className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-[#0e0f11] border border-[#1e2024] flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-[#00e5ff]" />
                <span className="font-bold text-white">{act.teamName}</span>
                <span className="text-zinc-400">{act.message}</span>
              </div>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-zinc-500 shrink-0">
                {formatTimeAgo(act.timestamp)}
              </span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

    </div>
  );
