=== FILE: ./src/components/CyberCursor.jsx ===
import React, { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react';

export const CyberCursor = () => {
  const [pos, setPos] = useState({ x: -100, y: -100 });
  const [targetPos, setTargetPos] = useState({ x: -100, y: -100 });
  const [isHovered, setIsHovered] = useState(false);
  const [isClicked, setIsClicked] = useState(false);
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(false);
  const [ripples, setRipples] = useState([]);
  const animRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Only enable on desktop devices with fine pointer
    if (window.matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches) {
      return;
    }

    const handleMouseMove = (e) => {
      setTargetPos({ x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });
      if (!isVisible) setIsVisible(true);

      // Check if target or parent is interactive
      const target = e.target;
      const isInteractive = Boolean(
        target.closest('button, a, input, select, textarea, [role="button"], label, .interactive')
      );
      setIsHovered(isInteractive);

      // Update card-level CSS custom properties for spotlight effects
      const card = target.closest('.spotlight-card');
      if (card) {
        const rect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
        const x = ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 100;
        const y = ((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 100;
        card.style.setProperty('--card-mouse-x', `${x}%`);
        card.style.setProperty('--card-mouse-y', `${y}%`);
      }
    };

    const handleMouseDown = (e) => {
      setIsClicked(true);
      // Create shockwave ripple
      const newRipple = {
        id: Date.now(),
        x: e.clientX,
        y: e.clientY
      };
      setRipples((prev) => [...prev.slice(-3), newRipple]);
      setTimeout(() => {
        setRipples((prev) => prev.filter((r) => r.id !== newRipple.id));
      }, 600);
    };

    const handleMouseUp = () => {
      setIsClicked(false);
    };

    const handleMouseLeave = () => {
      setIsVisible(false);
    };

    const handleMouseEnter = () => {
      setIsVisible(true);
    };

    window.addEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
    window.addEventListener('mousedown', handleMouseDown);
    window.addEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);
    document.addEventListener('mouseleave', handleMouseLeave);
    document.addEventListener('mouseenter', handleMouseEnter);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('mousemove', handleMouseMove);
      window.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleMouseDown);
      window.removeEventListener('mouseup', handleMouseUp);
      document.removeEventListener('mouseleave', handleMouseLeave);
      document.removeEventListener('mouseenter', handleMouseEnter);
      if (animRef.current) cancelAnimationFrame(animRef.current);
    };
  }, [isVisible]);

  // Smooth lerp (linear interpolation) for cursor ring
  useEffect(() => {
    let currentX = pos.x;
    let currentY = pos.y;

    const followLoop = () => {
      currentX += (targetPos.x - currentX) * 0.35;
      currentY += (targetPos.y - currentY) * 0.35;
      setPos({ x: currentX, y: currentY });
      animRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(followLoop);
    };

    animRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(followLoop);

    return () => {
      if (animRef.current) cancelAnimationFrame(animRef.current);
    };
  }, [targetPos]);

  if (!isVisible) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 pointer-events-none z-50 overflow-hidden">
      {/* 1. Large Atmospheric Glow Aura (Deep Violet & Electric Cyan) matching user image */}
      <div
        className="absolute rounded-full transition-opacity duration-300"
        style={{
          width: '320px',
          height: '320px',
          left: `${targetPos.x}px`,
          top: `${targetPos.y}px`,
          transform: 'translate(-50%, -50%)',
          background: isHovered
            ? 'radial-gradient(circle, rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.22) 0%, rgba(157, 78, 221, 0.18) 35%, rgba(6, 12, 32, 0.08) 65%, transparent 80%)'
            : 'radial-gradient(circle, rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.15) 0%, rgba(157, 78, 221, 0.12) 35%, rgba(6, 12, 32, 0.05) 65%, transparent 80%)',
          filter: 'blur(20px)',
        }}
      />

      {/* 2. Click Shockwave Ripples */}
      {ripples.map((r) => (
        <div
          key={r.id}
          className="absolute rounded-full border border-cyan-400 animate-ping opacity-75"
          style={{
            left: `${r.x}px`,
            top: `${r.y}px`,
            width: '60px',
            height: '60px',
            transform: 'translate(-50%, -50%)',
            boxShadow: '0 0 15px rgba(0, 240, 255, 0.8)'
          }}
        />
      ))}

      {/* 3. The Custom Tactical Cursor HUD Reticle (Exact replica of user reference) */}
      <div
        className="absolute transition-transform duration-150 ease-out"
        style={{
          left: `${pos.x}px`,
          top: `${pos.y}px`,
          transform: `translate(-50%, -50%) scale(${isClicked ? 0.88 : isHovered ? 1.25 : 1}) rotate(${isHovered ? '45deg' : '0deg'})`,
        }}
      >
        {/* SVG Tactical Reticle Ring & 4 Diagonal Amber Ticks */}
        <svg
          width="48"
          height="48"
          viewBox="0 0 48 48"
          fill="none"
          xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
          className="overflow-visible"
        >
          {/* Inner radial cyan gradient glow */}
          <circle
            cx="24"
            cy="24"
            r="16"
            fill="url(#inner-cyan-glow)"
          />

          {/* Cyan Circular Perimeter Ring */}
          <circle
            cx="24"
            cy="24"
            r="15"
            stroke="#00f0ff"
            strokeWidth="1.8"
            strokeOpacity="0.95"
            className="drop-shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(0,240,255,0.7)]"
          />

          {/* 4 Diagonal Amber/Orange Ticks at 45 degree angles (from user's image) */}
          {/* Top-Right Tick (45 deg) */}
          <line
            x1="35"
            y1="13"
            x2="41"
            y2="7"
            stroke="#ffb703"
            strokeWidth="2.2"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            className="drop-shadow-[0_0_6px_rgba(255,183,3,0.8)]"
          />

          {/* Top-Left Tick (135 deg) */}
          <line
            x1="13"
            y1="13"
            x2="7"
            y2="7"
            stroke="#ffb703"
            strokeWidth="2.2"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            className="drop-shadow-[0_0_6px_rgba(255,183,3,0.8)]"
          />

          {/* Bottom-Left Tick (225 deg) */}
          <line
            x1="13"
            y1="35"
            x2="7"
            y2="41"
            stroke="#ffb703"
            strokeWidth="2.2"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            className="drop-shadow-[0_0_6px_rgba(255,183,3,0.8)]"
          />

          {/* Bottom-Right Tick (315 deg) */}
          <line
            x1="35"
            y1="35"
            x2="41"
            y2="41"
            stroke="#ffb703"
            strokeWidth="2.2"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            className="drop-shadow-[0_0_6px_rgba(255,183,3,0.8)]"
          />

          {/* Center glowing white dot */}
          <circle
            cx="24"
            cy="24"
            r="3.2"
            fill="#ffffff"
            className="drop-shadow-[0_0_6px_#ffffff]"
          />

          <defs>
            <radialGradient
              id="inner-cyan-glow"
              cx="0"
              cy="0"
              r="1"
              gradientUnits="userSpaceOnUse"
              gradientTransform="translate(24 24) rotate(90) scale(15)"
            >
              <stop stopColor="#00f0ff" stopOpacity="0.3" />
              <stop offset="0.7" stopColor="#00f0ff" stopOpacity="0.1" />
              <stop offset="1" stopColor="#00f0ff" stopOpacity="0" />
            </radialGradient>
          </defs>
        </svg>
      </div>

      {/* 4. Instant Precise Dot Tracker (at exact target coordinates for precision feel) */}
      <div
        className="absolute w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-white shadow-[0_0_6px_#00f0ff]"
        style={{
          left: `${targetPos.x}px`,
          top: `${targetPos.y}px`,
          transform: 'translate(-50%, -50%)',
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { soundFx } from '../utils/sound';
import { 
  KeyRound, 
  Sparkles, 
  X
} from 'lucide-react';

export const AuthModal = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  teams,
  onLogin,
  onRegister
}) => {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState('login');
  
  // Login form
  const [selectedTeamId, setSelectedTeamId] = useState(teams[0]?.id || '');
  const [loginPasscode, setLoginPasscode] = useState('');
  const [loginError, setLoginError] = useState('');

  // Register form
  const [regName, setRegName] = useState('');
  const [regCollege, setRegCollege] = useState('NITK Surathkal');
  const [regMembers, setRegMembers] = useState('');
  const [regPasscode, setRegPasscode] = useState('');
  const [regAvatar, setRegAvatar] = useState('⚡');

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleLoginSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const team = teams.find((t) => t.id === selectedTeamId);
    if (!team) {
      setLoginError('Team not found');
      return;
    }
    // Check passcode if provided or allow demo login
    if (loginPasscode && team.passcode !== loginPasscode) {
      soundFx.playError();
      setLoginError('Incorrect team passcode.');
      return;
    }

    soundFx.playUnlock();
    onLogin(team);
    onClose();
  };

  const handleQuickDemoLogin = (team) => {
    soundFx.playUnlock();
    onLogin(team);
    onClose();
  };

  const handleRegisterSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!regName.trim() || !regPasscode.trim()) return;

    soundFx.playUnlock();
    onRegister({
      name: regName.trim(),
      college: regCollege.trim(),
      members: regMembers.split(',').map((m) => m.trim()).filter(Boolean),
      passcode: regPasscode.trim(),
      avatarSeed: regAvatar
    });
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md">
      <div className="relative max-w-lg w-full bg-[#141518] border border-cyan-500/50 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-[0_0_50px_rgba(0,240,255,0.25)]">
        
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-5 border-b border-slate-800 bg-[#0f1012]">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-cyan-500/20 border border-cyan-400 flex items-center justify-center text-[#00e5ff]">
              <KeyRound className="w-4 h-4" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white">Team Verification &amp; Login</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">ACM Vidyut &times; Engineer &apos;26 Hackathon</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={() => {
              soundFx.playClick();
              onClose();
            }}
            className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800 transition"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Tab switch */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 p-1.5 bg-slate-900/90 border-b border-slate-800 text-xs font-mono font-bold">
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              soundFx.playClick();
              setMode('login');
            }}
            className={`py-2 rounded-lg transition ${
              mode === 'login'
                ? 'bg-cyan-500 text-slate-950 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(0,240,255,0.4)]'
                : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
            }`}
          >
            Sign In Existing Team
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              soundFx.playClick();
              setMode('register');

            className={`py-2 rounded-lg transition ${
              mode === 'register'
                ? 'bg-cyan-500 text-slate-950 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(0,240,255,0.4)]'
                : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
            }`}
          >
            Register New Team
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Body */}

                    Team Passcode

                  <input
                    type="password"
                    value={loginPasscode}
                    onChange={(e) => setLoginPasscode(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Enter passcode (or leave blank for demo teams)"
                    className="w-full px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 text-white text-xs font-mono focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-400"
                  />
                </div>

                {loginError && (
                  <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-rose-950/60 border border-rose-600/60 text-xs text-rose-300">
                    {loginError}
                  </div>
                )}

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-400 to-blue-500 hover:from-cyan-300 hover:to-blue-400 text-slate-950 font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-mono shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(0,240,255,0.3)] transition"
                >
                  Confirm &amp; Access Dashboard
                </button>
              </form>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <form onSubmit={handleRegisterSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-mono text-slate-300 mb-1">
                  Team Name *
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={regName}
                  onChange={(e) => setRegName(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="e.g. QuantumCircuits_NITK"
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 text-white text-xs font-mono focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-400"
                />

                  placeholder="e.g. NITK Surathkal"
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 text-white text-xs font-mono focus:outline-none focus:border-cyan-400"

=== FILE: ./src/components/PuzzleCard.jsx ===
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { soundFx } from '../utils/sound';
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti';
import {
  CheckCircle2,
  Lock,
  Zap,
  HelpCircle,
  Code2,
  Copy,
  Check,
  AlertTriangle,
  KeyRound,
  ShieldCheck,
  FileCode2,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp
} from 'lucide-react';

const DIFF_STYLES = {
  STANDARD:     { pill: 'text-emerald-400 bg-emerald-950/40 border-emerald-800/40',  label: 'Standard' },
  INTERMEDIATE: { pill: 'text-cyan-400 bg-cyan-950/40 border-cyan-800/40',        label: 'Intermediate' },
  ADVANCED:     { pill: 'text-amber-400 bg-amber-950/40 border-amber-800/40',      label: 'Advanced' },
  EXPERT:       { pill: 'text-rose-400 bg-rose-950/40 border-rose-800/40',        label: 'Expert' },
};

export const PuzzleCard = ({
  puzzle,
  isUnlocked,
  isSolved,
  isNextToSolve,
  onSolve,
  onUseHint,
  hasUsedHint,
  activeTeam,
  onOpenAuth
}) => {
  const [inputAnswer, setInputAnswer] = useState('');
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState('');
  const [copiedCode, setCopiedCode] = useState(false);
  const [showHintConfirm, setShowHintConfirm] = useState(false);
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(true);

  const diff = DIFF_STYLES[puzzle.difficulty] || DIFF_STYLES.STANDARD;

  const handleSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!activeTeam) {
      onOpenAuth();
      return;
    }
    if (!inputAnswer.trim()) return;

    setIsSubmitting(true);
    setErrorMsg('');

    setTimeout(() => {
      const success = onSolve(puzzle.id, inputAnswer);
      if (success) {
        soundFx.playUnlock();
        confetti({
          particleCount: 85,
          spread: 75,
          origin: { y: 0.6 },
          colors: ['#00e5ff', '#fbbf24', '#ffffff', '#2dd4bf'],
        });
        setInputAnswer('');
      } else {
        soundFx.playError();
        setErrorMsg('Decryption Key mismatch. Double-check logic expressions and signal timing.');
      }
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }, 280);
  };

  const handleCopyCode = () => {
    if (puzzle.hardwareSnippet?.code) {
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(puzzle.hardwareSnippet.code);
      setCopiedCode(true);
      soundFx.playClick();
      setTimeout(() => setCopiedCode(false), 2000);
    }
  };

  const cardBorderClass = isSolved
    ? 'matte-card-solved'
    : isNextToSolve
    ? 'matte-card-active spotlight-card'
    : 'matte-card opacity-85';

  return (
    <div id={`stage-${puzzle.id}`} className={`${cardBorderClass} transition-all duration-200 overflow-hidden`}>
      
      {/* ── HEADER BAR ── */}
      <div className={`px-5 py-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b ${
        isSolved
          ? 'border-[#23272d] bg-[#101215]'
          : isNextToSolve
          ? 'border-[#2d313a] bg-[#16181c]'
          : 'border-[#1e2024] bg-[#111215]'
      }`}>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5 min-w-0">
          {/* Stage Icon */}
          <div className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center font-mono font-bold text-sm shrink-0 border ${
            isSolved
              ? 'bg-[#152427] border-[#00e5ff]/40 text-[#00e5ff]'
              : isNextToSolve
              ? 'bg-[#262115] border-amber-500/50 text-amber-300'
              : 'bg-[#18191c] border-[#25272c] text-zinc-500'
          }`}>
            {isSolved ? (
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4.5 h-4.5" />
            ) : isUnlocked ? (
              <span>P{puzzle.stageNumber}</span>
            ) : (
              <Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="flex items-center flex-wrap gap-2 mb-0.5">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500 font-bold">
                Stage 0{puzzle.stageNumber}
              </span>
              <span className={`text-[10px] font-mono px-2 py-0.5 rounded border font-semibold ${diff.pill}`}>
                {diff.label}
              </span>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-[#1c1d22] border border-[#2b2d35] text-zinc-400">
                {puzzle.category}
              </span>
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white tracking-tight truncate">
              {puzzle.title}
            </h3>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right Info: Points & Status */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
          <div className="text-right hidden sm:block font-mono">
            <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500 uppercase">Bounty</div>
            <div className="text-sm font-extrabold text-[#fbbf24]">
              +{puzzle.points} pts
            </div>
          </div>

          {isSolved && (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md text-xs font-mono font-bold bg-[#142327] border border-[#00e5ff]/40 text-[#00e5ff]">
              <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              DECRYPTED
            </span>
          )}

          {!isUnlocked && (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md text-xs font-mono text-zinc-500 bg-[#161719] border border-[#232427]">
              <Lock className="w-3 h-3" />
              LOCKED
            </span>
          )}

          {isSolved && (
            <button
              onClick={() => setExpanded(!expanded)}
              className="p-1.5 text-zinc-500 hover:text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-[#1a1b1f] transition"
            >
              {expanded ? <ChevronUp className="w-4 h-4" /> : <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4" />}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── CARD BODY ── */}
      {(!isSolved || expanded) && (
        <div className="p-5 sm:p-6 space-y-5">
          
          {/* Locked State Notice */}
          {!isUnlocked && (
            <div className="py-8 text-center space-y-2 border border-dashed border-[#23252a] rounded-xl bg-[#0f1012]">
              <Lock className="w-7 h-7 text-zinc-600 mx-auto" />
              <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-300">Stage Locked</div>
              <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 max-w-sm mx-auto">
                Decryption protocol locked. Complete <strong className="text-zinc-300">Stage 0{puzzle.id - 1}</strong> to unlock this puzzle and view its RTL netlist.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Unlocked / Solved Content */}
          {isUnlocked && (
            <>
              {/* Context / Lore */}
              <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-[#0e0f11] border border-[#1f2125] text-xs text-zinc-300 leading-relaxed font-sans">
                <span className="text-[#00e5ff] font-mono font-bold mr-2 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider">
                  [CONTEXT]:
                </span>
                {puzzle.storyContext}
              </div>

              {/* Problem Statement */}
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 font-bold">
                  <FileCode2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#00e5ff]" />
                  <span>Objective &bull; Decryption Goal</span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-sm text-zinc-100 font-medium leading-relaxed p-4 rounded-xl bg-[#101114] border border-[#23252a]">
                  {puzzle.puzzleStatement}
                </p>
              </div>

              {/* Code Snippet */}
              {puzzle.hardwareSnippet && (
                <div className="code-block-matte overflow-hidden">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-3.5 py-2 bg-[#121316] border-b border-[#1f2126] text-xs font-mono text-zinc-400">
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <Code2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#00e5ff]" />
                      {puzzle.hardwareSnippet.language.toUpperCase()} &bull; Hardware Netlist
                    </span>
                    <button
                      onClick={handleCopyCode}
                      className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] text-zinc-400 hover:text-white transition"
                    >
                      {copiedCode ? (
                        <>
                          <Check className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-400" />
                          <span className="text-emerald-400">Copied</span>
                        </>
                      ) : (
                        <>
                          <Copy className="w-3 h-3" />
                          <span>Copy</span>
                        </>
                      )}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                  <pre className="p-4 text-xs sm:text-[13px] font-mono text-zinc-200 overflow-x-auto leading-relaxed select-all">
                    <code>{puzzle.hardwareSnippet.code}</code>
                  </pre>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Signal Clue */}
              <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5 p-3 rounded-lg bg-[#14181f] border border-[#222834] text-xs text-zinc-300">
                <HelpCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-[#00e5ff] shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                <div>
                  <strong className="text-white">Signal Clue: </strong>
                  {puzzle.clue}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Hint Drawer */}
              <div>
                {hasUsedHint ? (
                  <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-[#1d1a13] border border-[#3d3319] text-xs text-amber-200 space-y-1 font-mono">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-bold text-amber-400 uppercase text-[11px]">
                      <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      UNLOCKED HINT (Penalty Incurred)
                    </div>
                    <p className="font-sans text-amber-200/90">{puzzle.hint}</p>
                  </div>
                ) : showHintConfirm ? (
                  <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-[#1c1913] border border-[#3d341a] flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs">
                    <div className="text-amber-200">
                      <strong className="text-amber-300">Unlock Hint?</strong> This will incur a{' '}
                      <span className="font-mono font-bold text-amber-400">+{puzzle.hintPenaltyMinutes}m</span>{' '}
                      timing penalty for your team.
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => setShowHintConfirm(false)}
                        className="px-3 py-1 rounded bg-[#252830] hover:bg-[#30333d] text-zinc-300"
                      >
                        Cancel
                      </button>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => {
                          soundFx.playZap();
                          onUseHint(puzzle.id);
                          setShowHintConfirm(false);
                        }}
                        className="px-3 py-1 rounded bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-400 text-[#0c0d0e] font-bold"
                      >
                        Confirm (+{puzzle.hintPenaltyMinutes}m)
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : !isSolved && (
                  <button
                    onClick={() => {
                      soundFx.playClick();
                      setShowHintConfirm(true);
                    }}
                    className="text-xs text-amber-400/80 hover:text-amber-300 inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-mono hover:underline"
                  >
                    <AlertTriangle className="w-3 h-3" />
                    Request Hardware Hint (+{puzzle.hintPenaltyMinutes}m penalty)
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Submission Form */}
              {!isSolved && (
                <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="pt-2 space-y-3">
                  <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2">
                    <div className="relative flex-1">
                      <KeyRound className="w-4 h-4 text-zinc-500 absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" />
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={inputAnswer}
                        onChange={(e) => setInputAnswer(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="Enter Decryption Key (e.g. signal name, hex token, operator)..."
                        className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-[#0a0b0d] border border-[#25272c] text-white placeholder-zinc-600 text-sm font-mono focus:outline-none focus:border-[#00e5ff] transition"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      disabled={isSubmitting || !inputAnswer.trim()}
                      className="btn-matte-primary disabled:opacity-40 disabled:cursor-not-allowed shrink-0"
                    >
                      {isSubmitting ? (
                        <span className="animate-spin text-base">⏳</span>
                      ) : (
                        <>
                          <Zap className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-current" />
                          <span>Submit Key</span>
                        </>
                      )}
                    </button>
                  </div>

                  {errorMsg && (
                    <div className="p-3 rounded-lg bg-[#241315] border border-rose-900/60 text-xs text-rose-300 flex items-center gap-2 animate-shake">
                      <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-400 shrink-0" />
                      <span>{errorMsg}</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </form>
              )}

              {/* Decrypted Fragment */}
              {isSolved && (
                <div className="mt-4 p-4 sm:p-5 rounded-xl bg-[#111619] border border-[#1e343d] space-y-3">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-[#1b3842] pb-2">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-[#00e5ff]" />
                      <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-[#00e5ff] uppercase tracking-wider">
                        DECRYPTED FRAGMENT 0{puzzle.fragment.index}/10: {puzzle.fragment.title}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-zinc-400 bg-[#0e1013] px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-[#23272e]">
                      CHECKSUM: {puzzle.fragment.checksum}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-zinc-200 leading-relaxed font-sans">
                    {puzzle.fragment.content}
                  </p>

                  {puzzle.fragment.verilogSignature && (
                    <div className="p-2.5 rounded bg-[#090a0c] border border-[#202b33] text-xs font-mono text-[#00e5ff] overflow-x-auto">
                      <code>{puzzle.fragment.verilogSignature}</code>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <div className="pt-2 text-xs text-zinc-400 border-t border-[#1a232b]">
                    <strong className="text-zinc-300">Engineering Rationale: </strong>
                    {puzzle.explanation}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </>
          )}

        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import ReactDOM from 'react-dom/client';
import App from './App.jsx';
import './index.css';

ReactDOM.createRoot(document.getElementById('root')).render(
  <React.StrictMode>
    <App />
  </React.StrictMode>
);


=== FILE: ./src/App.jsx ===
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { PUZZLES } from './data/puzzles';
import { INITIAL_TEAMS, INITIAL_ACTIVITY_LOGS } from './data/teams';
import { soundFx } from './utils/sound';

import { ElectricCanvas } from './components/ElectricCanvas';
import { CyberCursor } from './components/CyberCursor';
import { Navbar } from './components/Navbar';
import { HeroBanner } from './components/HeroBanner';
import { PuzzleStageList } from './components/PuzzleStageList';
import { LeaderboardView } from './components/LeaderboardView';
import { FinalProblemView } from './components/FinalProblemView';
import { LiveAnalysisView } from './components/LiveAnalysisView';
import { RulesView } from './components/RulesView';
import { AuthModal } from './components/AuthModal';
import { Footer } from './components/Footer';

export const App = () => {
  const [currentTab, setCurrentTab] = useState('puzzles');
  const [isAuthModalOpen, setIsAuthModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [soundEnabled, setSoundEnabled] = useState(soundFx.isEnabled());
  const [isSimulating, setIsSimulating] = useState(false);
  const [toastMessage, setToastMessage] = useState(null);

  // Initialize Teams from localStorage or fallback
  const [teams, setTeams] = useState(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('vq_teams_data');
    if (saved) {
      try {
        return JSON.parse(saved);
      } catch {
        // fallback
      }
    }
    return INITIAL_TEAMS;
  });

  // Current logged in team id
  const [activeTeamId, setActiveTeamId] = useState(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('vq_active_team_id');
    return saved !== null ? saved : 'team_bitflipper_01';
  });

  // Hints used by teams
  const [usedHintsMap, setUsedHintsMap] = useState(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('vq_used_hints');
    if (saved) {
      try { return JSON.parse(saved); } catch {}
    }
    return {};
  });

  // Activity feed
  const [activityLogs, setActivityLogs] = useState(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('vq_activity_logs');
    if (saved) {
      try { return JSON.parse(saved); } catch {}
    }
    return INITIAL_ACTIVITY_LOGS;
  });

  // Active Team Object
  const activeTeam = teams.find((t) => t.id === activeTeamId) || null;
  const currentTeamSolved = activeTeam ? activeTeam.solvedPuzzles : [];
  const activeTeamUsedHints = activeTeamId ? (usedHintsMap[activeTeamId] || []) : [];

  // Persist teams to localStorage
  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem('vq_teams_data', JSON.stringify(teams));
  }, [teams]);

  // Persist active team
  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeTeamId) {
      localStorage.setItem('vq_active_team_id', activeTeamId);
    } else {
      localStorage.removeItem('vq_active_team_id');
    }
  }, [activeTeamId]);

  // Persist used hints
  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem('vq_used_hints', JSON.stringify(usedHintsMap));
  }, [usedHintsMap]);

  // Persist activity logs
  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem('vq_activity_logs', JSON.stringify(activityLogs));
  }, [activityLogs]);

  // Toggle Sound FX
  const handleToggleSound = () => {
    const nextState = soundFx.toggle();
    setSoundEnabled(nextState);
  };

  // Toast notification helper
  const showToast = (msg) => {
    setToastMessage(msg);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setToastMessage(null);
    }, 4000);
  };

  // Solve Puzzle Handler
  const handleSolvePuzzle = (puzzleId, rawAnswer) => {
    if (!activeTeam) return false;

    const puzzle = PUZZLES.find((p) => p.id === puzzleId);
    if (!puzzle) return false;

    // Normalize answer: remove quotes, spaces, lowercase
    const cleanInput = rawAnswer.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/['"`]/g, '');
    const isCorrect = puzzle.acceptedAnswers.some((ans) => {
      const cleanAns = ans.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/['"`]/g, '');
      return cleanAns === cleanInput;
    });

    if (isCorrect) {
      // Check if already solved
      if (activeTeam.solvedPuzzles.includes(puzzleId)) {
        return true;
      }

      const now = Date.now();
      const updatedSolved = [...activeTeam.solvedPuzzles, puzzleId].sort((a, b) => a - b);
      const updatedScore = activeTeam.score + puzzle.points;
      const updatedTimestamps = {
        ...activeTeam.puzzleSolveTimestamps,
        [puzzleId]: now
      };

      const updatedTeam = {
        ...activeTeam,
        solvedPuzzles: updatedSolved,
        score: updatedScore,
        puzzleSolveTimestamps: updatedTimestamps,
        lastActive: now
      };

      setTeams((prev) => prev.map((t) => (t.id === activeTeam.id ? updatedTeam : t)));

      // Add to activity feed
      const newEvent = {
        id: `act_${Date.now()}`,
        timestamp: now,
        teamId: activeTeam.id,
        teamName: activeTeam.name,
        puzzleId: puzzle.id,
        puzzleTitle: puzzle.title,
        type: updatedSolved.length === 10 ? 'FINISH_ALL' : updatedSolved.length === 5 ? 'MILESTONE_5' : 'SOLVE',
        message: updatedSolved.length === 10 
          ? `🏆 ALL 10 STAGES COMPLETED! Transmitted Golden RTL Bitstream!` 
          : `Decrypted Stage 0${puzzle.id}! +${puzzle.points} pts awarded.`
      };

      setActivityLogs((prev) => [newEvent, ...prev.slice(0, 30)]);
      showToast(`⚡ Stage 0${puzzle.id} Decrypted! Fragment 0${puzzle.id} compiled.`);
      return true;
    }

    return false;
  };

  // Use Hint Handler
  const handleUseHint = (puzzleId) => {
    if (!activeTeam) return;
    const currentHints = usedHintsMap[activeTeam.id] || [];
    if (currentHints.includes(puzzleId)) return;

    const puzzle = PUZZLES.find((p) => p.id === puzzleId);
    if (!puzzle) return;

    const updatedHints = [...currentHints, puzzleId];
    setUsedHintsMap((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [activeTeam.id]: updatedHints
    }));

    const updatedTeam = {
      ...activeTeam,
      penaltyMinutes: activeTeam.penaltyMinutes + puzzle.hintPenaltyMinutes
    };

    setTeams((prev) => prev.map((t) => (t.id === activeTeam.id ? updatedTeam : t)));

    showToast(`⚠️ Hint unlocked (+${puzzle.hintPenaltyMinutes}m timing penalty).`);
  };

  // Switch / Login Team
  const handleLoginTeam = (team) => {
    setActiveTeamId(team.id);
    showToast(`Welcome back, ${team.name}!`);
  };

  // Register New Team
  const handleRegisterTeam = (newTeamData) => {
    const newId = `team_${Date.now()}`;
    const newTeam = {
      ...newTeamData,
      id: newId,
      solvedPuzzles: [],
      puzzleSolveTimestamps: {},
      penaltyMinutes: 0,
      score: 0,
      lastActive: Date.now()
    };

    setTeams((prev) => [newTeam, ...prev]);
    setActiveTeamId(newId);
    showToast(`Team "${newTeam.name}" registered successfully!`);
  };

  // Logout / Switch Team
  const handleLogout = () => {
    setActiveTeamId(null);
    showToast('Logged out of team profile.');
  };

  // Jump to next unsolved puzzle
  const handleJumpToCurrentPuzzle = () => {
    setCurrentTab('puzzles');
    const nextId = (currentTeamSolved.length + 1);
    setTimeout(() => {
      const el = document.getElementById(`stage-${nextId <= 10 ? nextId : 10}`);
      el?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
    }, 150);
  };

  // Jump to specific stage
  const handleJumpToStage = (stageId) => {
    setCurrentTab('puzzles');
    setTimeout(() => {
      const el = document.getElementById(`stage-${stageId}`);
      el?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
    }, 150);
  };

  // Live Competition Simulation Mode (Simulates competing teams making progress)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isSimulating) return;

    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      const competingTeams = teams.filter((t) => t.id !== activeTeamId && t.solvedPuzzles.length < 10);
      if (competingTeams.length === 0) return;

      const randomTeam = competingTeams[Math.floor(Math.random() * competingTeams.length)];
      const nextPuzzleToSolve = randomTeam.solvedPuzzles.length + 1;
      const puzzle = PUZZLES.find((p) => p.id === nextPuzzleToSolve);
      if (!puzzle) return;

      const now = Date.now();
      const updatedSolved = [...randomTeam.solvedPuzzles, puzzle.id];
      const updatedScore = randomTeam.score + puzzle.points;
      const updatedTimestamps = {
        ...randomTeam.puzzleSolveTimestamps,
        [puzzle.id]: now
      };

      const updated = {
        ...randomTeam,
        solvedPuzzles: updatedSolved,
        score: updatedScore,
        puzzleSolveTimestamps: updatedTimestamps,
        lastActive: now
      };

      setTeams((prev) => prev.map((t) => (t.id === randomTeam.id ? updated : t)));

      const newEvent = {
        id: `act_${now}`,
        timestamp: now,
        teamId: randomTeam.id,
        teamName: randomTeam.name,
        puzzleId: puzzle.id,
        puzzleTitle: puzzle.title,
        type: 'SOLVE',
        message: `Decrypted Stage 0${puzzle.id} (${puzzle.title})! Score: ${updatedScore} pts`
      };

      setActivityLogs((prev) => [newEvent, ...prev.slice(0, 30)]);
      showToast(`⚡ Live Update: ${randomTeam.name} solved Stage 0${puzzle.id}!`);
      soundFx.playZap();
    }, 14000);

    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, [isSimulating, teams, activeTeamId]);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#0c0d0e] text-zinc-100 flex flex-col relative selection:bg-[#00e5ff] selection:text-black">
      {/* Background Interactive Electric Canvas */}
      <ElectricCanvas />

      {/* Custom Tactical HUD Cursor Reticle (Matching User Reference Image) */}
      <CyberCursor />

      {/* Main App Navigation */}
      <Navbar
        currentTab={currentTab}
        onSelectTab={setCurrentTab}
        activeTeam={activeTeam}
        onOpenAuthModal={() => setIsAuthModalOpen(true)}
        onLogout={handleLogout}
        soundEnabled={soundEnabled}
        onToggleSound={handleToggleSound}
        solvedCount={currentTeamSolved.length}
        totalPuzzles={PUZZLES.length}
      />

      {/* Hero Banner with telemetry HUD & Countdown */}
      <HeroBanner
        activeTeam={activeTeam}
        solvedCount={currentTeamSolved.length}
        totalPuzzles={PUZZLES.length}
        onSelectTab={setCurrentTab}
        onOpenAuthModal={() => setIsAuthModalOpen(true)}
        onJumpToCurrentPuzzle={handleJumpToCurrentPuzzle}
      />

      {/* Dynamic Tab Views */}
      <main className="flex-1 relative z-10">
        {currentTab === 'puzzles' && (
          <PuzzleStageList
            puzzles={PUZZLES}
            activeTeam={activeTeam}
            onSolve={handleSolvePuzzle}
            onUseHint={handleUseHint}
            usedHints={activeTeamUsedHints}
            onOpenAuth={() => setIsAuthModalOpen(true)}
            onNavigateToFinal={() => setCurrentTab('final-problem')}
          />
        )}

        {currentTab === 'final-problem' && (
          <FinalProblemView
            puzzles={PUZZLES}
            activeTeam={activeTeam}
            onJumpToStage={handleJumpToStage}
            onOpenAuth={() => setIsAuthModalOpen(true)}
          />
        )}

        {currentTab === 'leaderboard' && (
          <LeaderboardView
            teams={teams}
            puzzles={PUZZLES}
            activeTeam={activeTeam}
            activityLogs={activityLogs}
            isSimulating={isSimulating}
            onToggleSimulation={() => setIsSimulating(!isSimulating)}
          />
        )}

        {currentTab === 'analysis' && (
          <LiveAnalysisView
            teams={teams}
            puzzles={PUZZLES}
            activeTeam={activeTeam}
          />
        )}

        {currentTab === 'rules' && (
          <RulesView />
        )}
      </main>

      {/* Footer */}
      <Footer />

      {/* Team Authentication Modal */}
      <AuthModal
        isOpen={isAuthModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsAuthModalOpen(false)}
        teams={teams}
        onLogin={handleLoginTeam}
        onRegister={handleRegisterTeam}
      />

      {/* Floating Toast Notification */}
      {toastMessage && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-5 right-5 z-50 p-4 rounded-xl bg-[#16171b] border border-[#2a2d35] text-xs font-mono text-zinc-200 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-md flex items-center gap-3 animate-bounce">
          <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-[#00e5ff] animate-ping"></span>
          <span>{toastMessage}</span>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default App;


=== FILE: ./src/data/teams.js ===
const BASE_START_TIME = Date.now() - 3 * 3600 * 1000; // 3 hours ago

export const INITIAL_TEAMS = [
  {
    id: "team_rtl_overclockers",
    name: "RTL_Overclockers",
    college: "NITK Surathkal",
